生命、攻击、防御等由等级、装备与强化计算。可通过上方对应字段调整；这里不覆盖战斗公式。||Health, attack, defense and more are computed from level, equipment and upgrades. Adjust them via the fields above; this does not override combat formulas.
物品数量设为 0 会移除持有；强化需要持有法杖。已学卡牌至少保留 1 份，升级会按原规则学习法术，降级保留已学卡牌。||Setting an item count to 0 removes it; upgrading requires holding the wand. Learned cards keep at least 1 copy; leveling up learns spells by the original rules, and leveling down keeps learned cards.

# js/view_adventure_fishing.js
海面。点击鱼影撒网，或使用下方撒网按钮对准金色光圈。||The sea. Click a fish shadow to cast, or use the cast button below to aim at the golden circle.

撒网||Cast net
普通网要罩住鱼影才结算。必中网和捕鱼器点中海面就会结算。渔获、扣网和扣精力都按原版兑换表，鱼的种类不由你点中的影子决定。||A normal net must cover a fish shadow to resolve. Sure-hit nets and fish catchers resolve on any sea click. Catch, net loss and stamina use follow the original redemption table; the fish type is not decided by the shadow you hit.

魔法星如何成长||How the Magic Star grows
剩余有效期按北京时间的日历天数自动兑换为魔豆，每天 10 颗。第一次从今天算到到期日；记下这次兑到的日期后，再次兑换只计算更晚的新增天数。||Remaining term auto-exchanges into magic beans by Beijing-time calendar days, 10 per day. The first exchange counts from today to the expiry date; note the date it reaches, and later exchanges count only newly added days after that.
续期后点击“刷新会员状态”，查看最新等级。新增的会员天数会自动兑换。||After renewing, click "Refresh membership status" to see the latest level. Newly added membership days exchange automatically.
有效日期暂时无法确认时，已确认的会员按 1 级显示。||When valid dates cannot be confirmed yet, confirmed members show as level 1.

在营地认识五系导师，沿道路开始初心旅程。||Meet the five mentors in camp and start the beginner journey along the road.
沿林间训练道路寻找侦察兵。||Follow the forest training road to find the scouts.
营地中央的五系导师广场，沿道路向北进入训练林地。||The five mentors' square in the center of camp. Follow the road north into the training grove.
营地南侧的道路尽头，通往哈奇小镇与世界地图。||The end of the road on the south side of camp, leading to Haqi Town and the world map.
北方的雪峰与山脚林地连成一线。沿山路向东，可以望见金色的山谷。||The northern snow peaks run into the woods at their foot. Follow the mountain road east to see the golden valley.
林间小路弯向雪山，河岸的岔路则通往南边田野。留意远离大路的怪物。||The forest path bends toward the snow peaks, and the fork by the river leads to the southern fields. Watch for monsters off the main road.
金色岩坡隔开了北方山地和东部海岸。沿南坡绕行，可以回到小镇。||Golden rock slopes separate the northern mountains from the east coast. Follow the south slope back to town.
农田之间留着宽阔的田埂。过桥是小镇，沿河向南则是一片安静的湖岸。||Wide ridges run between the fields. Cross the bridge for town, or follow the river south to a quiet lakeshore.
河水在这里汇成湖泊。水泡怪在远处的岸边活动，主路上可以安心经过。||The river gathers into a lake here. Bubble creatures wander the far shore; the main road is safe to pass.
熟悉的小镇是探索的起点。西行过桥去田野，北行通往山谷，东行可到海滨。||The familiar town is where exploration starts. Cross the bridge west to the fields, go north to the valley, or east to the shore.
海风从东边吹来。原版地图上的游乐区域位于这一侧，专属设施和故事将逐步补全。||The sea breeze comes from the east. The original map's amusement area is on this side; its rides and stories will be filled in over time.
从小镇走到海边，沿弯曲的沙滩回望山林。营地传送门就在小镇南侧。||Walk from town to the sea and look back at the woods along the curving beach. The camp portal is just south of town.
西南海岸是登岛起点，北行通向高地，东侧道路绕过熔岩。||The southwest coast is the landing. North leads to the heights, and the east road goes around the lava.
火山山脊在这里分成两条路，一条通向北部，一条绕行东南。||The volcanic ridge splits here: one road goes north, the other loops southeast.
原图北方的金色巨树是这片高地的视觉中心。||The golden great tree from the original northern art is the landmark of these heights.
环道绕开炽热熔岩，桥头连接东部岩坡。||The loop road stays clear of the hot lava, and the bridge joins the eastern rock slope.
熔岩占据东南低地，沿标记的桥梁与石路行进。||Lava fills the southeast lowland. Stay on the marked bridges and stone road.
西部高低岩台与狭窄小路形成另一条回程路线。||The high and low rock terraces in the west, with a narrow path, make another way back.
从南部港湾踏上浮冰通道，北方雪峰指引前进方向。||Step from the south harbor onto the floe passage. The northern snow peaks show the way.
冰块之间留有深水，沿冰桥前往东侧雪原。||Deep water lies between the ice. Follow the ice bridge to the snowfield on the east.
西部背风坡仍有松林，从这里可绕回海港。||Pines still stand on the sheltered western slope. From here you can loop back to the harbor.
积雪覆盖北方山岭，山路连接西部林地和东部高地。||Snow covers the northern ridge. The mountain road joins the western woods and the eastern heights.
高地的冰蓝山峰与雪道构成寒冰岛的远景。||Ice-blue peaks and snow tracks make the distant view of Ice Island.
开阔冰原与浮冰通道相连，南行可以返回港湾。||The open ice field joins the floe passage. South leads back to the harbor.
南部水源是旅程的起点，向北进入峡谷，向西可到遗迹。||The southern water is the start of the journey. North enters the canyon, and west reaches the ruins.
西侧伸入海中的石台与古老遗迹由狭路连接。||A narrow path joins the stone terrace that reaches into the sea with the ancient ruins.
峡谷中的道路分向西侧遗迹、北部沙丘和东方高地。||Roads in the canyon split toward the western ruins, the north dunes, and the eastern heights.
开阔沙丘围绕北方小水源，远处的岩壁标示道路边界。||Open dunes surround the small northern spring. The far rock wall marks the edge of the road.
东北独立高台经曲折坡道抵达，参考原图高地和古建筑的关系。||The standalone northeast mesa is reached by a winding ramp, following the original heights and ruins.
东南岩谷保留回环路线，可沿南坡返回绿洲。||The southeast rock valley keeps a loop. Follow the south slope back to the oasis.
南方旧广场是幽暗岛入口，穿过山谷道路前往中央林地。||The old southern square is the entrance to Dark Isle. The valley road leads to the central woods.
岩壁与树影包围中央谷地，岔路通向不同高地。||Rock walls and tree shadows enclose the central hollow. The forks lead to different heights.
西北红色险地参考原图的独立环形地标，沿外环经过。||The red northwest hazard follows the original ring landmark. Stay on the outer loop.
北方森林延伸至高地，薄雾中仍可沿路牌返回。||The northern forest climbs toward the heights. The signposts still lead back through the mist.
东侧地标眺望外海，曲折道路连接中央山谷。||The eastern landmark looks out to sea. A winding road joins the central valley.
东北石脊提供另一条连通北部密林的道路。||The northeast stone ridge is another road into the northern woods.

# data/adventure/chapter.json
任务63000–63013按编号串联，去除等级上限锁；地图距离缩短。||Quests 63000–63013 are chained by number, without the level-cap lock; map distances are shorter.
旧版视角、传送和教学演示改为浏览器操作；原对白与逐条改编记录均保存在章节数据。||The old camera, teleport and tutorial demos are browser controls; the original dialogue and each adaptation note stay in the chapter data.
毕业后来到哈奇小镇；原任务63014前往火鸟岛，未纳入本章。||After graduation you reach Haqi Town; the original quest 63014 to Firebird Island is not part of this chapter.
1–10级经验阈值按原任务奖励编排；技能解锁时间为本章安排。||Experience thresholds for levels 1–10 follow the original quest rewards; skill unlock timing is arranged for this chapter.
原出奇蛋随机结果在本章固定为水咕噜；宠物仅跟随、喂养，不参战。||The original Surprise Egg's random result is fixed as a water Gululu in this chapter; pets only follow and can be fed, and do not fight.
VIP仙豆礼盒保留为纪念收藏，不开放充值或随机兑换。||The VIP fairy-bean gift box is kept as a souvenir and does not open top-up or random exchange.
帽子、衣服、鞋子奖励保留本系选项；桌面鼠标提示同步适配触摸。||Hat, clothes and shoe rewards keep your school's options; desktop mouse hints are adapted for touch.
部分营地居民共用原版立绘，外观资源映射不改变NPC身份。||Some camp residents share original portraits; the art mapping does not change who the NPC is.
初始基础牌每种3份；装备附带法术作为额外固定手牌补充。||Each starter basic card has 3 copies; spells attached to equipment are extra fixed cards in hand.
胜负后恢复生命；无耐久损耗、在线计时、会员或节日暴怒机制。||HP recovers after a win or loss; there is no durability loss, online timer, membership or holiday rage.

# data/adventure/chapter.json
欢迎来到魔法营地！我是青龙。从这里开始，学会你的第一道魔法吧。||Welcome to Magic Camp! I'm Azure Dragon. Let's start your journey by learning your first spell.
我来试试||Let me try!
点一下地面就能走过去，点一下居民就能上前交谈。先在营地里试试吧！||Click the ground to walk there, or click a resident to walk over and talk. Try it here in camp!
迈出第一步了！接下来，去认识营地里的朋友，一起发现魔法的乐趣。||You've taken your first step! Now meet your friends around camp and discover the joys of magic together.
谢谢你||Thank you!
请指教||Please teach me.
魔法让我快乐！哪怕只是一个小法术，也能给平常的日子添点惊喜。你也去试试吧。||Magic makes me happy! Even a little spell can bring a touch of wonder to an ordinary day. Give it a try!
记住了||Got it!
我学习魔法，是想变得更强，守护身边的人。遇到困难时，有力量就能多帮上一把。||I study magic to grow stronger and protect the people around me. With that strength, I can lend a hand when times get tough.
去问问苏苏和莫卡，他们为什么学习魔法。打开任务，点击“追踪目标”就能找到他们。||Ask Susu and Moka why they study magic. Open Quests and click "Track objectives" to find them.
有人为快乐，有人为守护。带着你自己的心愿，我们开始练习魔法吧！||Some learn for joy, others to protect people. Whatever your own wish may be, let's start practicing magic!
去挑战烈火侦查兵。战斗时，先点卡牌，再点目标，就能施法；魔力不足时可以跳过回合。||Challenge the Fire Scout. In battle, click a card, then a target to cast your spell. You can pass a turn if you don't have enough pips.
第一场胜利！这顶帽子送给你。打开“背包”穿上它，还能用上装备附带的卡牌。||Your first victory! Here's a hat for you. Open your Bag to equip it and use the cards it grants.
这次挑战寒冰侦查兵，试试刚学会的本领。完成训练后，我送你一件新衣服。||Challenge the Ice Scout this time and put your new skills to the test. Finish the training, and I'll give you a new outfit.
打得不错！收好这件衣服，记得到“背包”里穿上，为下一场挑战做好准备。||Well fought! Take this outfit and remember to equip it in your Bag. Get ready for your next challenge!
营地受光明守护，侦察兵是能自行修复的训练伙伴。放心挑战风暴侦察兵吧！||The light protects our camp, and the scouts are training partners that repair themselves. Go ahead and challenge the Storm Scout!
你越来越熟练了！这双鞋送给你。训练伙伴很快就能恢复，不必担心它们。||You're getting better! These shoes are for you. Don't worry about your training partners; they'll recover soon.
接下来是精英生命侦查兵，试试你的实力吧！获胜后去找古奇，为出行准备装备。||The Elite Life Scout is next. Show what you can do! After you win, visit Goochi to get ready for your journey.
听说你通过了训练，干得好！想去更远的地方冒险，先准备一把趁手的法杖吧。||I heard you passed your training. Well done! Before you venture farther afield, you'll need a trusty wand.
去找青龙领取法杖吧。有了趁手的武器，再学会强化，你就能更从容地迎接挑战。||Go see Azure Dragon to get your wand. With a trusty weapon and a little knowledge of upgrading, you'll be ready for tougher challenges.
这把法杖送给你！接下来，我会教你强化装备，让它在冒险中助你一臂之力。||This wand is yours! Next, I'll teach you to upgrade your equipment so it can serve you better on your adventures.
打开“背包”的“强化装备”，选一件可强化的装备完成强化，再回来找我。记得备好仙豆！||Open "Upgrade equipment" in your Bag, choose an item you can upgrade, and upgrade it. Then come back to me. Bring enough Fairy beans!
强化成功了！送你一枚出奇蛋。打开它，看看哪位小伙伴会加入你的旅程吧。||Upgrade complete! Here's a Surprise Egg. Open it and see which little friend will join your journey!
打开“宠物”，孵化出奇蛋，再去找杰西卡。她会教你照顾这位新伙伴。||Open Pet and hatch your Surprise Egg, then visit Jessica. She'll teach you how to care for your new companion.
瞧，你们已经成了好朋友！在“宠物”里可以查看伙伴、喂它口粮，陪它一起长大。||Look, you're already friends! Open Pet to check on your companion, feed it, and watch it grow.
打开“宠物”，点击“喂养一包战宠口粮”。让新伙伴吃饱，它就有精神陪你冒险啦！||Open Pet and click "Feed a pack of pet food". A full tummy will give your new friend the energy to adventure with you!
小家伙吃得真开心！记得照顾它，往后的旅程，你们就能一路相伴了。||Your little friend loved that meal! Take good care of it, and you'll have a companion for the adventures ahead.
烈火热情而执着，擅长用火焰攻击多个敌人。面对成群的对手，让火焰为你开路吧！||Fire is passionate and determined, using flames to strike several enemies at once. When foes gather, let fire clear your path!
寒冰的力量在于坚韧。稳住防守，承受对手的攻势，再抓住机会还击。||Ice draws its strength from endurance. Hold your defenses, weather the enemy's attacks, then seize your chance to strike back.
风暴追求强力的单体攻击。看准关键对手，集中力量出击，让战斗尽快分出胜负！||Storm excels at powerful attacks against a single target. Pick a key opponent, focus your power, and bring the battle to a swift end!
生命魔法能恢复生命、支援伙伴。照顾好自己和同伴，才能一起走得更远。||Life magic restores health and supports your allies. Care for yourself and your companions, and you'll go farther together.
死亡魔法能在攻击时吸取生命。削弱对手，也为自己争取继续战斗的机会。||Death magic drains life as it attacks. Weaken your enemies while giving yourself the strength to keep fighting.
去见见营地里的五位魔法导师吧。每一系都有自己的拿手本领，听听他们怎么说。||Meet the five magic mentors around camp. Each school has its own strengths. Hear what they have to say!
各系各有所长，配合卡牌才能发挥本领。打开“卡包”查看已学魔法，调整后记得保存。||Each school has its strengths, and the right mix of cards brings them out. Open Cards to review your spells, and save any changes you make.
死亡侦察兵会在攻击时吸血。别让它慢慢恢复，准备好卡牌，抓住机会尽快击败它！||The Death Scout drains life when it attacks. Don't let it keep healing! Prepare your cards and defeat it as quickly as you can.
最终考核快到了。先去找领地看守者，打听对手的本领，做好准备再挑战。||Your final test is coming up. Visit the Territory keeper to learn about your opponent's abilities, then prepare before taking the challenge.
知道对手会吸血，就更要安排好进攻。收好这个新卡包，接下来整理一下你的卡牌。||Since your opponent can drain life, you'll need to plan your attacks carefully. Take this new card bag, then organize your cards.
在“背包”装备翡翠口袋，再打开“卡包”调整卡牌。可以点“推荐配卡”，最后记得保存。||Equip the Emerald Pocket in your Bag, then open Cards to adjust your cards. You can click "Recommended". Remember to save!
准备得不错！出发前整理卡包，是魔法师的好习惯。现在，迎接最后的考核吧。||You're well prepared! Every good magician checks their deck before setting out. Now it's time for your final test.
最后一关：击败死亡侦察兵！带上准备好的卡牌，去证明你已经能独当一面吧。||Your final challenge: defeat the Death Scout! Take your prepared deck and show that you can stand on your own.
恭喜你，通过考核！带上伙伴和勇气出发吧，营地之外，还有更多奇妙的相遇等着你。||Congratulations, you passed! Set out with your companion and your courage. More wonderful encounters await beyond the camp!

一名敌人||one enemy
全体友方||all allies
全体敌人||all enemies
全场所有角色||everyone in the arena
伤害为目标当前生命的 {percent}%||Damage: {percent}% of the target's current HP
对{target}造成其当前生命 {percent}% 的基础伤害，另受此牌的伤害上限限制。||Deal base damage equal to {percent}% of the current HP of {target}, subject to this card's damage cap.
每段基础 {amount}||Base per hit: {amount}
对{target}立即造成 {amount} 点{school}基础伤害。||Immediately deal {amount} base {school} damage to {target}.
分段攻击 {times} 次；特殊段数可能使用不同伤害。||Attack {times} times. Special hits may use different damage values.
持续 {ticks}||Damage over time: {ticks}
持续治疗 {ticks}||Healing over time: {ticks}
每回合 {amount}||{amount} each turn
各回合依次为 {amounts}||{amounts} on successive turns
为{target}附加持续治疗：{rounds} 回合，{schedule} 点基础生命值。||Apply healing over time to {target} for {rounds} turns: {schedule} base HP.
对{target}附加持续伤害：{rounds} 回合，{schedule} 点基础伤害。||Apply damage over time to {target} for {rounds} turns: {schedule} base damage.
立即为{target}恢复 {amount} 点基础生命值。||Immediately restore {amount} base HP to {target}.
吸收 {amount}||Absorb {amount}
为{target}施加护盾，累计吸收 {amount} 点伤害。护盾不会恢复已经损失的生命。||Give {target} a shield that absorbs a total of {amount} damage. It does not restore lost HP.
吸收就是替你承受伤害：结算到护盾的伤害先扣护盾容量，超过剩余容量的部分才扣生命；没用完的容量保留，用完后护盾消失。||Absorption takes damage for you: damage reaching the shield uses its capacity first. Only damage exceeding its remaining capacity reduces HP. Unused capacity stays; the shield disappears when depleted.
例如：护盾剩余 {capacity} 点，承受一次 200 点伤害后，生命不减少，护盾还剩 {remaining} 点。||Example: with {capacity} shield capacity left, taking 200 damage costs no HP and leaves {remaining} shield capacity.
基础总伤害：{amount} 点（即时效果与持续效果合计，并非一次生效）。||Total base damage: {amount}, combining immediate damage and damage over time, not all at once.
基础总治疗：{amount} 点（即时效果与持续效果合计，并非一次生效）。||Total base healing: {amount}, combining immediate healing and healing over time, not all at once.
{from}伤害→{to}伤害||{from} damage → {to} damage
给{target}放置棱镜。当该目标下一次受到{from}伤害时，将这次伤害转换为{to}伤害，并消耗棱镜；其他系伤害不会触发这个棱镜。||Place a prism on {target}. The next time that target receives {from} damage, convert that damage to {to} and consume the prism. Other damage schools do not trigger this prism.
转换的是目标受到的伤害，不是目标发出的攻击，也不会改变卡牌或角色的学系。转换后按新学系的抗性结算。||The prism changes incoming damage, not the target's outgoing attacks or the school of a card or character. Resistance is then checked against the new school.
用途：如果敌人抵抗{from}、却不擅长抵抗{to}，先放棱镜，再用{from}攻击它。伤害不保证增加，仍取决于它对两系的抗性。||Use it when an enemy resists {from} more than {to}: place the prism, then attack with {from}. The damage increase depends on its resistances to both schools.
棱镜与护盾、陷阱按后放先处理的顺序结算；搭配使用时，放置顺序会影响哪些效果触发。||Prisms, shields and traps are processed newest first. When combining them, placement order affects which effects trigger.

减伤盾按比例减少一次匹配的伤害；吸收盾则有固定容量，可以分多次扣除。||A damage-reduction shield reduces one matching damage event by a percentage. An absorb shield has a fixed capacity that can cover multiple hits.
